---
title: "aws_lexv2models_intent"
description: "Real, generated bindings for `aws_lexv2models_intent`."
---

`aws_lexv2models_intent` -- real, typed bindings generated directly from the
`hashicorp/aws` provider schema, in every SDK language.

Every tab below is a complete, runnable program, not a fragment,
real enough to save and run exactly as shown.

## Example

<Tabs>
  <Tab title="Go">
    ```go
    package main

    import (
    	lexv2models "github.com/ubiquex/ubx-sdk-aws/sdk/go/aws/lexv2models"
    	ubx "github.com/ubiquex/ubx-sdk-go/runtime"
    )

    func main() {
    	ubx.Main(ubx.Stack("payments", func() {
    		ubx.Intent(ubx.IntentInfo{Summary: "payments own intent"})

    		ubx.Resource(lexv2models.Intent, "ci-runner", lexv2models.IntentConfig{
    			BotId:                 "example",
    			BotVersion:            "example",
    			LocaleId:              "example",
    			Name:                  "ci-runner",
    			Description:           "Managed by ubx.",
    			ParentIntentSignature: "example",
    		})
    	}))
    }
    ```
  </Tab>
  <Tab title="TypeScript">
    ```typescript
    import { intent, resource, stack } from "@ubx/sdk";
    import { Intent } from "jsr:@ubx/sdk-aws/aws/lexv2models/intent";

    export default stack("payments", () => {
      intent({ summary: "payments own intent" });

      resource(Intent, "ci-runner", {
        botId: "example",
        botVersion: "example",
        localeId: "example",
        name: "ci-runner",
        description: "Managed by ubx.",
        parentIntentSignature: "example",
      });
    });
    ```
  </Tab>
  <Tab title="Python">
    ```python
    import ubx_sdk as ubx
    from ubx.aws.lexv2models import Intent, IntentConfig

    def describe():
        ubx.intent("payments own intent")

        ubx.resource(Intent, "ci-runner", IntentConfig(
            bot_id="example",
            bot_version="example",
            locale_id="example",
            name="ci-runner",
            description="Managed by ubx.",
            parent_intent_signature="example",
        ))

    if __name__ == "__main__":
        ubx.run("payments", describe)
    ```
  </Tab>
  <Tab title="Markdown">
    ```
    Create aws_lexv2models_intent called "ci-runner", with bot id
    example, bot version example, locale id example, description
    Managed by ubx., parent intent signature example.
    ```
  </Tab>
</Tabs>
